review_text: Went to this coffee shop awhile back on a girls trip in Orange Beach. The restaurant is connected to Hotel Indigo, but there is parking right outside of the coffee shop as well. If you are in the area and in search of a good breakfast/brunch spot…look no further.  Breakfast menu has a wide selection and there’s enough there to choose from to satisfy the pickiest of eaters. We ate here almost every day for breakfast and each time, we were highly satisfied with the service and quality of food. They had so many delicious coffee drinks to choose from, and drink customizations included milk alternatives and decaf coffee as well. There are booths and tables inside, but outside dining is also available and it’s clean. Overall, the experience eating here was outstanding, and I will definitely be back when vacationing in the area again.

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 42ec1d99-109c-4259-997c-5cc57336e9fe
entity_slug: the-southern-grind-coffee-house-at-the-wharf
reviewer_name: Reni
rating: 5
body: Went to this coffee shop awhile back on a girls trip in Orange Beach. The restaurant is connected to Hotel Indigo, but there is parking right outside of the coffee shop as well. If you are in the area and in search of a good breakfast/brunch spot…look no further.  Breakfast menu has a wide selection and there’s enough there to choose from to satisfy the pickiest of eaters. We ate here almost every day for breakfast and each time, we were highly satisfied with the service and quality of food. They had so many delicious coffee drinks to choose from, and drink customizations included milk alternatives and decaf coffee as well. There are booths and tables inside, but outside dining is also available and it’s clean. Overall, the experience eating here was outstanding, and I will definitely be back when vacationing in the area again.
